# Agents · P · part 27

928 entries, prd-creator to preparer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **prd-creator**: Use this agent when you need to create a comprehensive Product Requirements Document (PRD) based on customer requirements or business needs. Examples include: 1) A product manager wants to document new feature requirements after receiving customer feedback, 2) A development team needs a structured PRD to begin implementation of a new product initiative, 3) An organization wants to formalize requirements before starting a product development cycle. The agent should be used proactively when product requirements need to be documented in a standardized format. ([jscoobyced/jsc-claude-dev-env](https://github.com/jscoobyced/jsc-claude-dev-env/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prd-creator.md))
- **prd-creator**: Creates a Product Requirements Document (PRD) for a new feature based on user input. The PRD is a detailed document that guides junior builders on what to build and why. The agent will ask questions to gather necessary information before generating the PRD. ([luizarnoldch/code-automation](https://github.com/luizarnoldch/code-automation/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/prd-creator.agent.md))
- **prd-creator**: Product Requirements Document（PRD）を作成する専門エージェント。ビジネス要件を構造化し、ユーザー価値と成功指標を定義します。 ([mitarashi077/ExamPreparationSystem](https://github.com/mitarashi077/ExamPreparationSystem/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prd-creator.md))
- **prd-creator**: Use this agent when you need to create a Product Requirements Document (PRD) for a new feature, enhancement, or system component. This includes analyzing requirements, defining business objectives, documenting technical constraints, and establishing success criteria. ([Omcodes23/speedrun-arbitrum-vibekit](https://github.com/Omcodes23/speedrun-arbitrum-vibekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prd-creator.md))
- **prd-creator-agent**: 访谈式 agent，以增量方式逐 section 构建高质量 PRD。 向用户提出相关问题，在推进前评估每个 section 的质量， 并将结果保存到 docs/features/&lt;feature-slug&gt;/prd.md。 ([bianc2018/AgentForge](https://github.com/bianc2018/AgentForge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prd-creator-agent.md))
- **prd-creator-agent**: Agente entrevistador que constrói um PRD de alta qualidade de forma incremental, seção por seção. Faz perguntas pertinentes ao usuário, avalia a qualidade de cada seção antes de avançar e salva o resultado em docs/features/&lt;feature-slug&gt;/prd.md. ([dfialho84/atritoboutique](https://github.com/dfialho84/atritoboutique/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prd-creator-agent.md))
- **prd-critic**: Rubric-score a PRD pack against the 7-dimension PM Brain rubric, run a 4-persona panel review, identify the single weakest section, and propose a concrete rewrite. Read-only — does NOT modify the PRD. Use when critiquing 02-product-requirements.md and 03-success-metrics.md in a pm-brain feature hub. ([ertra/pm-brain-for-cursor](https://github.com/ertra/pm-brain-for-cursor/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/prd-critic.md) · ★23)
- **prd-critic**: Parameterized PRD critic agent -- evaluates PRDs from one of four perspectives (business, technical, security, edge-case) and produces severity-rated findings. ([matthew-plusprogramming/monorepo](https://github.com/matthew-plusprogramming/monorepo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prd-critic.md) · 2 copies)

Grades the PRD against a fixed checklist and returns PASS or FAIL with the exact fixes needed. - **prd-curator**: PRD 문서(PRD.md 및 docs/prd/ 하위 문서)를 생성·갱신·유지보수하는 다국어(한국어/English/日本語/中文) 전문 에이전트. Lingual 앱의 기능을 추가·수정·삭제할 때 항상 먼저 호출해 PRD를 단일 진실 공급원(Source of Truth)으로 유지한다. 사용 시점은 (1) 사용자가 기능 추가/변경/삭제/재설계를 요청할 때, (2) 요구사항·수용 기준·스코프가 불분명할 때, (3) 구현 전에 PRD 반영 여부를 확인해야 할 때. - **prd-drafter**: Use this agent when the user asks for a feature spec, PRD, one-pager, or product requirements doc for a MeetFlow feature. - **prd-drafter**: Read-only drafter for one section range of a long brief. Never numbers REQs, never writes files, never writes the register. ([Deepta-AI/bearing](https://github.com/Deepta-AI/bearing/blob/HEAD/plugins/bearing/agents/prd-drafter.md) · ★1)

- **premortem**: Observer for delegated work. - **premortem**: Imagines the project has failed three months from now and works backward to find the likely causes, writing reviews/premortem.md. Use proactively before a launch, release, store submission, big refactor or migration. ([ISenseHostility/jarno-plugins](https://github.com/ISenseHostility/jarno-plugins/blob/HEAD/premortem/agents/premortem.md))
- **premortem**: Pre-execution plan attacker. Use PROACTIVELY between planning and execution on any T3 task — after a worker (or the main agent) has produced its AUQ, step plan, and gates, but before substantive work begins. Given the brief and the plan artifacts, it assumes the deliverable already failed, writes the most plausible postmortems, and converts them into a small set of concrete plan amendments. Cheap and fast by design; minutes, not hours. Never executes the plan. Not for reviewing finished work — that is mega-opus-redteam's job. ([weisberg/dotclaude](https://github.com/weisberg/dotclaude/blob/HEAD/agents/premortem.md))
- **premortem-agent**: Imagine the incident a shipped work unit would cause, working backward to find the missing guard ([rakovi4/continue-example](https://github.com/rakovi4/continue-example/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/premortem-agent.md) · ★2)
- **premortem-agent**: Attacks one idea in execution, in a fresh context that did not generate it — the baselines it must beat and whether they run, the field's own metric, what the evaluation plan depends on, and a three-state verdict on whether three months of this would produce a number. Judges execution, never novelty. Dispatched by the premortem skill, up to five at a time. ([rbh227/research-bearings](https://github.com/rbh227/research-bearings/blob/HEAD/agents/premortem-agent.md) · ★1)

- **preop-judge**: Pre-op plan judge — the FIRST gate of the /scientific-method harness, pinned to Fable. Reviews an empirical test PLAN before it runs — does the method actually DISCRIMINATE H1 from H0 and the named confounds, are the controls real, is the decision rule pre-committed and falsifiable — and returns APPROVED / APPROVED-WITH-REVISIONS / REJECTED. Judges the METHOD from the pasted packet, not the repo. No approval, no run. ([Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim](https://github.com/Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/preop-judge.md))
- **preop-judge**: The first gate in the /scientific-method harness, pinned to Fable. Examines an empirical test plan before it is executed — can this method actually separate the hypothesis from the null and from each named confound, are the controls genuine rather than assumed, is the decision rule committed in advance and capable of being falsified. Emits APPROVED / APPROVED-WITH-REVISIONS / REJECTED. Judges the method from the packet it is handed, not by exploring the repository. Nothing runs without approval. ([maxwellcsutton/agent-validation-harness](https://github.com/maxwellcsutton/agent-validation-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/preop-judge.md))

- **prepare-swoole-release**: Use this agent when the user wants to prepare this project's PHP stubs for a new, stable Swoole release, e.g. "prepare stubs for Swoole 6.1.0" or "bring the ide-helper stubs up to date with Swoole 6.0.3". Give it the target Swoole version. It diffs swoole-src between the version this project currently supports and the target version, updates only the stubs affected by that diff, wholesale-replaces src/swoole_library/ from the matching swoole/library release, brings README.md's derived parts (version examples, PHP configuration settings, build options, minimum PHP version) up to date, and leav… ([swoole/ide-helper](https://github.com/swoole/ide-helper/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/prepare-swoole-release.md) · ★519)
